<lessons_from_build_3>
Apply these to every script you write.
- Check elevation with [Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal]::IsInRole([Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator), never the string 'Administrator'. That string asks for a group that does not exist and fails in an elevated shell.
- Make destructive steps reversible: move to D:\Quarantine\<name>-review-<date> instead of deleting, and delete only regenerable caches. A script that bulk-deletes can be blocked by the harness; a script that quarantines is accepted and safer.
- Dry run first, always, and print a planned total. Test every wildcard filter in a dry run: the filter .wslconfig.* also matched the live .wslconfig. Prefer a regex on Name plus an explicit exclusion.
- Check for running processes before touching an app's folder, and say what to close.
- A silent exit code is not a result: print exit codes (1605 on an MSI uninstall means already gone, 3010 means reboot).
- Opam and Cygwin files carry the System attribute: deleting them needs takeown, icacls, attrib -s and cmd rd with the \\?\ prefix from an elevated shell. Long node_modules paths break Get-ChildItem; use the \\?\ prefix.
- PowerShell 5.1: no ternary, no &&, Set-Content writes CRLF and a BOM, Get-AppxPackage -AllUsers throws without elevation (wrap it in try/catch and fail safe).
</lessons_from_build_3>

<design>
Apply every rule to every script.
- One manifest drives everything: Old Laptop Rebuild\Windows Toolkit\toolkit.json. Each tool has id, display name, category, install method (winget id or other), optional version pin, machines (both, dell, acer), install-location policy, verify command, expected-version pattern, owned config files, and use-case fields (purpose, when to reach for it, key commands, where it runs, agent notes). The install, verify and catalog scripts read this one file. Install policies: install, latest, verify, absent.
- Scripts in Windows Toolkit\_scripts\: 00-preflight.ps1, 10-install-toolkit.ps1, 20-apply-configs.ps1, 40-build-catalog.ps1, 99-verify.ps1 and a README with run order and rollback. Add scripts only when a step cannot live in one of these.
- Every script: ASCII only, safe to run twice, -DryRun, detects the machine and the vault root (environment variable first, then candidate paths that contain AGENTS.md), logs to D:\WSL\ops\windows\logs\, writes a result JSON to Windows Toolkit\results\<host>\, never prints secrets, never deletes except replacing a config after a timestamped backup, exits non-zero on a required failure, continues past one failed package and prints a summary table.
- Installs use winget: --exact --accept-package-agreements --accept-source-agreements --disable-interactivity, and --location D:\Apps\<Name> only when the installer supports it. Record each tool's size and drive. Machine-wide installers raise UAC; say so first. Honour the Build 3 layout contract.
- The Dell run records versions in toolkit.lock.json; the Acer run installs those versions where winget has them.
- Configs are written once into Windows Toolkit\configs\ as UTF-8 without BOM and LF using [IO.File]::WriteAllText, copied by the apply script, with tools pointed at the shared location through user environment variables, and a SHA-256 table. "Identical" means the hash matches the source.
- Machine differences are explicit guarded blocks, never edited copies. The Dell's profile keeps its opam, Kiro and New-PyEnv blocks.
- Checks that mean something: parse every script with [System.Management.Automation.Language.Parser]::ParseFile, dry-run each, and let 99-verify.ps1 compare the manifest with reality (version matches, config hash, env vars, font family registered, profile loads in a new shell, Starship renders).
Design notes carried over from the earlier session, verify before use: no winget package exists for the font, so use Nerd Fonts v3.5.1 IosevkaTerm.tar.xz (about 87.5 MB, sha256 [REDACTED], check the published SHA-256.txt), extract only the IosevkaTermNerdFontMono-* TTFs with bsdtar, install per user (%LOCALAPPDATA%\Microsoft\Windows\Fonts plus an HKCU registry entry); it registers as "IosevkaTerm NFM", verify through System.Drawing.Text.InstalledFontCollection. The PowerShell 7 Windows Terminal profile GUID is {[REDACTED]}. Verify the Tokyo Night Storm palette from folke/tokyonight extras. Profile design: one managed block between markers (UTF-8 output, STARSHIP_CONFIG, Starship init guarded by $env:STARSHIP_SHELL, a once-only zoxide init, the VS Code hook behind Test-Path ~/.config/vscode-env/init.ps1 when $env:TERM_PROGRAM -eq 'vscode'). Terminal settings: merge a shared fragment (defaults, scheme, actions, keybindings) into each machine's settings.json and preserve per-machine profiles. Machine PATH precedes user PATH, so the pip uv in C:\Python313\Scripts wins until Python is removed.
</design>

### Contradictions logged (the live machine wins)

1. **The Acer snapshot is stale.** `VERSIONS.md` and `MANIFEST.md` exist now, though the findings note said they did not. The vault notes ([[VS Code - Windows]], [[Installations]], the Install Loop) say that on 2026-10-12 the Acer got these:
   - PowerShell 7 and 5.1 profiles at `D:\_Anant\20_Progress\Documents\{PowerShell,WindowsPowerShell}\`, holding Starship, zoxide, UTF-8 and the VS Code hook.
   - fzf, fd, bat, delta and zoxide, with PSFzf, a bat config and an fd ignore file.
   - delta in the Windows `.gitconfig`.
   - A standalone uv 0.12.19.
   - VS Code on `D:\Apps`.

   The Acer's Windows side is therefore ahead of its snapshot. None of those profile or config files are in the vault.
2. **The pip `uv` is redundant to remove by hand.** `04-admin-remove.ps1 -IncludePython` deletes `C:\Python313` entirely, so the separate `pip uninstall uv` in D2 is unnecessary.
3. **winget portables may not reach PATH.** The Acer note shows that `fzf`, `fd`, `bat`, `delta` and `zoxide` installed through winget but had no PATH entry until hard-linked into `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Microsoft\WinGet\Links`. The install script must check `Get-Command` after each portable and repair this.
4. **Terminal action ids differ.** The Dell's Terminal uses `User.*` action ids and the Acer's uses `Terminal.*`. The shared fragment will define explicit actions that work on both.
5. **Codex is outdated.** The Dell's Windows Codex is 0.160.0 (npm); winget lists the Store app as 26.1007.

| D15 (new) Corepack and pnpm | `corepack enable` needs admin under Program Files. **Use winget `pnpm.pnpm` and leave Corepack disabled.** |
| D16 (new) Starship location | **Copy the file to `~\.config\starship.toml` (hash-checked) and set `STARSHIP_CONFIG` to that path.** This avoids depending on the vault being reachable when a shell opens. The master copy stays in `Windows Toolkit\configs\`. |

### 3. Scripts and run order

All go in `Windows Toolkit\_scripts\`. Each is ASCII-only, safe to run twice, takes `-DryRun`, logs to `D:\WSL\ops\windows\logs\`, writes a result JSON to `results\<host>\`, and none needs an elevated shell. The installers raise UAC prompts for Git, gh, Starship and Node, and I will announce them first.

1. `00-preflight.ps1` is read-only. It detects the machine, the vault and the profile paths, compares the manifest with reality, and secret-scans every config it will write.
2. `10-install-toolkit.ps1` installs per the manifest, including the font, and runs the portable PATH repair.
3. `20-apply-configs.ps1` applies profiles, Starship, the Terminal merge, git and env vars, with timestamped backups and a SHA-256 table.
4. `99-verify.ps1` runs the real checks. On the Dell it writes `toolkit.lock.json`.
5. `40-build-catalog.ps1` runs in Phase 5.

Plus `_lib.ps1` (**proposed addition**), dot-sourced by all five, for machine and vault detection, logging, manifest reading and result writing. Without it that code would be copied five times. There is also a `README.md` with the run order and rollback, and a `toolkit.json` manifest. `30-wsl-config-parity.sh` is dropped from this build (Build 5 owns WSL).

By-hand steps are the Node 22 to LTS swap, `04-admin-remove.ps1 -IncludePython` from an elevated shell, the one visual glyph check, and the `agy` "Install in PATH" click.

### 4. Catalog outline

`catalog\Tool Catalog - <host>.md` and `tool-catalog-<host>.json` have these parts:
- A header with host, drives, time and manifest hash.
- A table per category: tool, version, path, drive, config, purpose, when to use, key commands, where it runs.
- An "unmanifested" table.
- A WSL section, read only.
- MCP servers by name with "command resolves". On the Dell, Claude Desktop lists jarvis-fs, github, jarvis and the-plan, and VS Code lists context7, github, firecrawl, the-plan and jarvis. Codex lists jarvis, jarvis-fs, node_repl and cua_repl. `~\.claude.json` did not parse in my quick read, so the script will handle it more carefully.
- Config files with hashes.

The authored note "Machine Toolkit - Tool Use Cases and Agent Guide" comes after that, next to Installations.

2. `00-preflight`.
3. `10-install-toolkit`.
4. `20-apply-configs`.
5. `99-verify`.
6. `40-build-catalog`.

## Phase 3 is done: scripts, manifest and configs are written, tested and waiting for you to run them

Nothing on the Dell has been changed yet. The real installs, the font registration, the git and environment writes, the Node swap and Starship rendering can't be proven by a dry run. Step 1 below is the first real test of them, and the outputs will show whether each one worked.

All 16 decisions (D1 to D16) and the `_lib.ps1` helper are built in. Everything is in the vault under `Old Laptop Rebuild\Windows Toolkit\`. `_scripts\README.md` has the run order and rollback, and the findings note has the full Phase 3 log.

### What I tested
- **Syntax:** all 7 scripts parse clean and contain no non-ASCII bytes.
- **Dry runs:** `00-preflight` (a real run, read-only), plus `10`, `20`, `11` and `40` in dry-run mode, all exit 0. The preflight plans 15 installs or upgrades, flags Node 22 for the hand swap, and flags Python and Chocolatey for later removal.
- **Terminal merge:** tested on a copy of the Dell's settings and on the Acer's snapshot. Both end with Tokyo Night Storm, `IosevkaTerm NFM`, PowerShell 7 as default and Ubuntu renamed per machine. The Acer's conda profiles survive, and a second run changes nothing.
- **Profile block:** tested on a copy of the Dell's real profile (opam, Kiro and `New-PyEnv` kept) and on a worst-case Acer-style profile. Duplicate Starship, zoxide, PSFzf and VS Code lines get commented out, the BOM and CRLF are kept, and the result parses. A profile with two statements on one line is left untouched and reported as MANUAL.
- **Downloads:** the font archive hash matches the published list and extracts exactly 27 TTFs (about 380 MB, not the 60 MB I first estimated). The uv 0.12.19 installer hash is pinned.
- **Bugs fixed on the way:** 9 bugs found by the tests and fixed, all listed in the findings note.

Every replaced file is backed up to `D:\Backup\Toolkit\<timestamp>\` first.

    { "id": "git-ignore", "kind": "copy", "source": "configs\\git\\ignore", "target": "%USERPROFILE%\\.config\\git\\ignore", "onExisting": { "dell": "replace", "acer": "replace" }, "sha256": "[REDACTED]" },
    { "id": "git-delta", "kind": "copy", "source": "configs\\git\\delta.gitconfig", "target": "%USERPROFILE%\\.config\\git\\delta.gitconfig", "onExisting": { "dell": "replace", "acer": "replace" }, "requiresTool": "delta" },
    { "id": "bat", "kind": "copy", "source": "configs\\bat\\config", "target": "%APPDATA%\\bat\\config", "onExisting": { "dell": "replace", "acer": "keep" }, "note": "Reconstructed from Installations; the Acer keeps its real file until the Acer paste-back is promoted." },
    { "id": "fd-ignore", "kind": "copy", "source": "configs\\fd\\ignore", "target": "%APPDATA%\\fd\\ignore", "onExisting": { "dell": "replace", "acer": "keep" }, "note": "Reconstructed from Installations; the Acer keeps its real file until the Acer paste-back is promoted." },

```
# _lib.ps1 - shared helpers for the Windows Toolkit scripts.
# ASCII only. Works in Windows PowerShell 5.1 and PowerShell 7. Dot-source it:  . "$PSScriptRoot\_lib.ps1"
# No ternary, no &&, no 2>&1 on native commands (they go through Invoke-Native).

$global:TKLibDir = $PSScriptRoot
$global:TK = $null
$global:TKBlockBegin = '# >>> TOOLKIT MANAGED BLOCK (Build 4) >>>'
$global:TKBlockEnd = '# <<< TOOLKIT MANAGED BLOCK (Build 4) <<<'
$global:TKUtf8NoBom = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)
$global:TKGenericVersion = '(\d+\.\d+(?:\.\d+)*)'
$global:TKSecretPatterns = @(
    'AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}',
    'gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{30,}',
    'github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{30,}',
    'sk-[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}',
    'xox[baprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}',
    '-----BEGIN[ A-Z]*PRIVATE KEY-----',
    'AIza[0-9A-Za-z\-_]{35}',
    'ya29\.[0-9A-Za-z\-_]+',
    '(?i)\b(api[_-]?key|secret|passwd|password)\b\s*[=:]\s*[''"]?[A-Za-z0-9/_\-\.]{8,}',
    '(?i)Bearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9\-_\.]{15,}'
)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- basics
function Test-IsAdmin {
    $id = [Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent()
    return ([Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal]$id).IsInRole([Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)
}

function ConvertTo-Ascii {
    param([string]$Text)
    if ($null -eq $Text) { return '' }
    return ([regex]::Replace($Text, '[^\x20-\x7E\r\n\t]', '?'))
}

function Expand-ToolkitPath {
    param([string]$Path, [string]$VaultRoot)
    if (-not $Path) { return $Path }
    $p = $Path
    if ($p -match '\{MyDocuments\}') { $p = $p.Replace('{MyDocuments}', [Environment]::GetFolderPath('MyDocuments')) }
    if ($p -match '\{vaultRoot\}') { $p = $p.Replace('{vaultRoot}', $VaultRoot) }
    return [Environment]::ExpandEnvironmentVariables($p)
}

function Update-SessionPath {
    $m = [Environment]::ExpandEnvironmentVariables([string][Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable('Path', 'Machine'))
    $u = [Environment]::ExpandEnvironmentVariables([string][Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable('Path', 'User'))
    $env:Path = (($m, $u) | Where-Object { $_ }) -join ';'
}

function Read-JsonLoose {
    param([string]$Path)
    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Web.Extensions
    $ser = New-Object System.Web.Script.Serialization.JavaScriptSerializer
    $ser.MaxJsonLength = [int]::MaxValue
    $ser.RecursionLimit = 200
    return $ser.DeserializeObject([IO.File]::ReadAllText($Path))
}

function Write-Utf8NoBom {
    param([string]$Path, [string]$Text, [switch]$KeepEol)
    $dir = Split-Path -Parent $Path
    if ($dir -and -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null }
    $t = $Text
    if (-not $KeepEol) { $t = $t.Replace("`r`n", "`n") }
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($Path, $t, $global:TKUtf8NoBom)
}

function Get-FileSha256 {
    param([string]$Path)
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Path -PathType Leaf)) { return $null }
    return (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToUpper()
}

function Get-TextSha256 {
    param([string]$Text)
    $sha = [Security.Cryptography.SHA256]::Create()
    $bytes = $global:TKUtf8NoBom.GetBytes($Text)
    return (($sha.ComputeHash($bytes) | ForEach-Object { $_.ToString('X2') }) -join '')
}

function Get-SecretHitCount {
    param([string]$Text)
    $n = 0
    if (-not $Text) { return 0 }
    foreach ($p in $global:TKSecretPatterns) { if ($Text -match $p) { $n++ } }
    return $n
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- machine and vault
function Find-VaultRoot {
    $cands = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
    if ($env:TOOLKIT_VAULT_ROOT) { $cands.Add($env:TOOLKIT_VAULT_ROOT) }
    $u = [Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable('TOOLKIT_VAULT_ROOT', 'User')
    if ($u) { $cands.Add($u) }
    $d = $global:TKLibDir
    for ($i = 0; $i -lt 10 -and $d; $i++) {
        $cands.Add($d)
        $d = Split-Path -Parent $d
    }
    $cands.Add('D:\Users\_Anant\10_Areas\Documents\Jarvis')
    $cands.Add('D:\_Anant\20_Progress\Documents\Jarvis')
    try { $cands.Add((Join-Path ([Environment]::GetFolderPath('MyDocuments')) 'Jarvis')) } catch { }
    foreach ($c in $cands) {
        if ($c -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $c 'AGENTS.md'))) { return $c }
    }
    return $null
}

function Get-MachineRole {
    param($Manifest, [string]$VaultRoot, [string]$Override)
    if ($Override) { return $Override }
    foreach ($name in 'dell', 'acer') {
        $mv = $Manifest.machines.$name.vaultRoot
        if ($mv -and $VaultRoot -and ($VaultRoot -ieq $mv)) { return $name }
    }
    if ($env:COMPUTERNAME -ieq $Manifest.machines.dell.computerName) { return 'dell' }
    return $null
}

function Test-ToolOnMachine {
    param($Tool, [string]$Role)
    return (@($Tool.machines) -contains $Role)
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- init, log, results
function Initialize-Toolkit {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Name, [bool]$DryRun = $false, [string]$Machine)
    $root = Split-Path -Parent $global:TKLibDir
    $manifestPath = Join-Path $root 'toolkit.json'
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $manifestPath)) { throw "toolkit.json not found at $manifestPath" }
    $manifest = Get-Content -LiteralPath $manifestPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
    $vault = Find-VaultRoot
    $role = Get-MachineRole -Manifest $manifest -VaultRoot $vault -Override $Machine
    if (-not $role) { throw 'Cannot tell which laptop this is. Re-run with -Machine dell or -Machine acer.' }
    $logDir = 'D:\WSL\ops\windows\logs'
    try { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $logDir | Out-Null } catch { $logDir = $env:TEMP }
    $ts = Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss'
    $resDir = Join-Path (Join-Path $root 'results') $env:COMPUTERNAME
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $resDir | Out-Null
    $global:TK = @{
        Name = $Name; Root = $root; ManifestPath = $manifestPath; Manifest = $manifest
        Vault = $vault; Role = $role; Host = $env:COMPUTERNAME; Ts = $ts; DryRun = $DryRun
        LogFile = (Join-Path $logDir ("toolkit-{0}-{1}.log" -f $Name, $ts))
        ResultsDir = $resDir; Items = (New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object])
        Started = Get-Date; Backups = (New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object])
        ManifestHash = (Get-FileSha256 $manifestPath)
    }
    Update-SessionPath
    Write-Log ("=== {0} on {1} ({2}) role={3} dryRun={4} admin={5}" -f $Name, $env:COMPUTERNAME, $manifest.machines.$role.label, $role, $DryRun, (Test-IsAdmin)) 'STEP'
    Write-Log ("Toolkit root: {0}" -f $root)
    Write-Log ("Vault root:   {0}" -f $vault)
    Write-Log ("Log file:     {0}" -f $global:TK.LogFile)
    if ($DryRun) { Write-Log '*** DRY RUN: nothing will be changed ***' 'WARN' }
    return $global:TK
}

function Write-Log {
    param([string]$Message, [string]$Level = 'INFO')
    $m = ConvertTo-Ascii $Message
    $line = ('{0} [{1}] {2}' -f (Get-Date -Format 'HH:mm:ss'), $Level, $m)
    $color = 'Gray'
    if ($Level -eq 'ERROR') { $color = 'Red' }
    elseif ($Level -eq 'WARN') { $color = 'Yellow' }
    elseif ($Level -eq 'OK') { $color = 'Green' }
    elseif ($Level -eq 'STEP') { $color = 'Cyan' }
    Write-Host $line -ForegroundColor $color
    if ($global:TK -and $global:TK.LogFile) {
        try { [IO.File]::AppendAllText($global:TK.LogFile, $line + [Environment]::NewLine, $global:TKUtf8NoBom) } catch { }
    }
}

function Add-Result {
    param([string]$Id, [string]$Status, [string]$Detail = '', [hashtable]$Extra)
    $o = [ordered]@{ id = $Id; status = $Status; detail = (ConvertTo-Ascii $Detail) }
    if ($Extra) { foreach ($k in $Extra.Keys) { $o[$k] = $Extra[$k] } }
    $global:TK.Items.Add([pscustomobject]$o)
    $lvl = 'INFO'
    if ($Status -eq 'FAIL') { $lvl = 'ERROR' }
    elseif ($Status -in 'WARN', 'DIFF', 'MANUAL', 'PENDING') { $lvl = 'WARN' }
    elseif ($Status -in 'PASS', 'OK', 'INSTALLED', 'UPGRADED', 'CHANGED', 'UNCHANGED') { $lvl = 'OK' }
    Write-Log ("{0,-8} {1,-22} {2}" -f $Status, $Id, $Detail) $lvl
}

function Complete-Toolkit {
    param([hashtable]$Summary)
    $items = $global:TK.Items
    $counts = @{}
    foreach ($i in $items) { if ($counts.ContainsKey($i.status)) { $counts[$i.status]++ } else { $counts[$i.status] = 1 } }
    Write-Host ''
    Write-Host ('{0,-24} {1,-9} {2}' -f 'ID', 'STATUS', 'DETAIL')
    foreach ($i in $items) {
        $d = [string]$i.detail
        if ($d.Length -gt 110) { $d = $d.Substring(0, 107) + '...' }
        Write-Host ('{0,-24} {1,-9} {2}' -f $i.id, $i.status, $d)
    }
    Write-Host ''
    $cs = ($counts.Keys | Sort-Object | ForEach-Object { '{0}={1}' -f $_, $counts[$_] }) -join '  '
    Write-Log ("SUMMARY: {0}" -f $cs) 'STEP'
    $fails = 0
    if ($counts.ContainsKey('FAIL')) { $fails = $counts['FAIL'] }
    $res = [ordered]@{
        script = $global:TK.Name; host = $global:TK.Host; machine = $global:TK.Role; dryRun = $global:TK.DryRun
        started = $global:TK.Started.ToString('s'); finished = (Get-Date).ToString('s')
        admin = (Test-IsAdmin); manifestSha256 = $global:TK.ManifestHash; vaultRoot = $global:TK.Vault
        log = $global:TK.LogFile; counts = $counts; items = @($items)
    }
    if ($Summary) { foreach ($k in $Summary.Keys) { $res[$k] = $Summary[$k] } }
    $json = ConvertTo-Json -InputObject ([pscustomobject]$res) -Depth 12
    $stamp = Join-Path $global:TK.ResultsDir ("{0}-{1}.json" -f $global:TK.Name, $global:TK.Ts)
    $latest = Join-Path $global:TK.ResultsDir ("{0}-latest.json" -f $global:TK.Name)
    if ($global:TK.DryRun) {
        $stamp = Join-Path $global:TK.ResultsDir ("{0}-dryrun-{1}.json" -f $global:TK.Name, $global:TK.Ts)
        $latest = Join-Path $global:TK.ResultsDir ("{0}-dryrun-latest.json" -f $global:TK.Name)
    }
    Write-Utf8NoBom -Path $stamp -Text $json
    Write-Utf8NoBom -Path $latest -Text $json
    Write-Log ("Result JSON: {0}" -f $stamp)
    if ($fails -gt 0) { Write-Log ("{0} required failure(s). Exit code 1." -f $fails) 'ERROR'; exit 1 }
    Write-Log 'No required failures. Exit code 0.' 'OK'
    exit 0
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- native commands
function Format-NativeArg {
    param([string]$A)
    if ($null -eq $A) { return '""' }
    if ($A -eq '') { return '""' }
    if ($A -notmatch '[\s"]') { return $A }
    return '"' + $A.Replace('"', '\"') + '"'
}

function Invoke-Native {
    param([string]$File, [string[]]$Arguments = @(), [int]$TimeoutSec = 120, [hashtable]$Env)
    $ext = [IO.Path]::GetExtension($File).ToLower()
    $args2 = @($Arguments)
    $exe = $File
    if ($ext -eq '.ps1') {
        $exe = (Get-Command powershell.exe).Source
        $args2 = @('-NoProfile', '-ExecutionPolicy', 'Bypass', '-File', $File) + @($Arguments)
    }
    $psi = New-Object System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
    $psi.FileName = $exe
    $psi.Arguments = (($args2 | ForEach-Object { Format-NativeArg $_ }) -join ' ')
    $psi.UseShellExecute = $false
    $psi.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
    $psi.RedirectStandardError = $true
    $psi.RedirectStandardInput = $true
    $psi.CreateNoWindow = $true
    $psi.StandardOutputEncoding = [Text.Encoding]::UTF8
    $psi.StandardErrorEncoding = [Text.Encoding]::UTF8
    if ($Env) { foreach ($k in $Env.Keys) { $psi.EnvironmentVariables[$k] = [string]$Env[$k] } }
    try {
        $p = [System.Diagnostics.Process]::Start($psi)
    } catch {
        return [pscustomobject]@{ ExitCode = -2; Output = ''; Error = $_.Exception.Message; TimedOut = $false }
    }
    try { $p.StandardInput.Close() } catch { }
    $outTask = $p.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync()
    $errTask = $p.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync()
    if (-not $p.WaitForExit($TimeoutSec * 1000)) {
        try { $p.Kill() } catch { }
        return [pscustomobject]@{ ExitCode = -1; Output = ''; Error = 'timeout'; TimedOut = $true }
    }
    $p.WaitForExit()
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ExitCode = $p.ExitCode; Output = $outTask.Result; Error = $errTask.Result; TimedOut = $false }
}

function Invoke-PSCode {
    # Runs code in a fresh PowerShell via -EncodedCommand. $Exe defaults to Windows PowerShell 5.1.
    param([string]$Code, [int]$TimeoutSec = 90, [switch]$NoProfile, [string]$Exe)
    $b64 = [Convert]::ToBase64String([Text.Encoding]::Unicode.GetBytes($Code))
    $e = $Exe
    if (-not $e) { $e = (Get-Command powershell.exe).Source }
    $a = @('-NoLogo', '-NonInteractive')
    if ($NoProfile) { $a += '-NoProfile' }
    $a += @('-EncodedCommand', $b64)
    return (Invoke-Native -File $e -Arguments $a -TimeoutSec $TimeoutSec)
}

function Resolve-CmdAll {
    param([string]$Name)
    $all = @(Get-Command $Name -All -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Where-Object { $_.CommandType -eq 'Application' -or $_.CommandType -eq 'ExternalScript' })
    return @($all | ForEach-Object { $_.Source })
}

function Resolve-Cmd {
    param([string]$Name)
    $all = Resolve-CmdAll $Name
    if ($all.Count -eq 0) { return $null }
    return $all[0]
}

function Write-NativeTail {
    param($R, [int]$Lines = 12)
    $text = (ConvertTo-Ascii (($R.Output + "`n" + $R.Error)))
    $keep = @($text -split "`r?`n" | ForEach-Object { $_.Trim() } | Where-Object { $_ -and $_ -notmatch '^[-\\|/ ]+$' })
    $tail = $keep | Select-Object -Last $Lines
    foreach ($l in $tail) { Write-Log ("    | {0}" -f $l) }
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- winget
function Get-WingetInstalledVersion {
    param([string]$Id)
    $w = Resolve-Cmd 'winget'
    if (-not $w) { return $null }
    $r = Invoke-Native -File $w -Arguments @('list', '--id', $Id, '--exact', '--accept-source-agreements', '--disable-interactivity') -TimeoutSec 120
    foreach ($line in ($r.Output -split "`r?`n")) {
        if ($line -match ('(^|\s)' + [regex]::Escape($Id) + '(\s|$)')) {
            $cols = @($line -split '\s{2,}')
            for ($i = 0; $i -lt $cols.Count; $i++) {
                if ($cols[$i].Trim() -ieq $Id -and ($i + 1) -lt $cols.Count) { return ($cols[$i + 1].Trim() -replace '^[<>~= ]+', '') }
            }
        }
    }
    return $null
}

function Invoke-WingetAction {
    # Action: install | upgrade | uninstall. Returns the Invoke-Native result.
    param([string]$Action, [string]$Id, [string]$Version)
    $w = Resolve-Cmd 'winget'
    $a = @($Action, '--id', $Id, '--exact', '--source', 'winget', '--accept-source-agreements', '--disable-interactivity')
    if ($Action -ne 'uninstall') { $a += '--accept-package-agreements' }
    if ($Version -and $Action -ne 'uninstall') { $a += @('--version', $Version) }
    if ($Action -eq 'uninstall') { $a += '--silent' }
    Write-Log ("winget {0}" -f ($a -join ' '))
    $r = Invoke-Native -File $w -Arguments $a -TimeoutSec 1800
    Write-NativeTail $r
    Write-Log ("winget exit code {0}" -f $r.ExitCode)
    return $r
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- tool state
function Get-ToolVersionFromExe {
    param([string]$Path, $Tool)
    $va = @('--version')
    if ($Tool.versionArgs) { $va = @($Tool.versionArgs) }
    $r = Invoke-Native -File $Path -Arguments $va -TimeoutSec 40
    $text = ($r.Output + ' ' + $r.Error)
    $rx = $global:TKGenericVersion
    if ($Tool.versionRegex) { $rx = $Tool.versionRegex }
    $m = [regex]::Match($text, $rx)
    if ($m.Success) { return $m.Groups[1].Value }
    return $null
}

function Test-FontFamily {
    param([string]$Family)
    $code = "Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing; `$f = (New-Object System.Drawing.Text.InstalledFontCollection).Families | Where-Object { `$_.Name -eq '$Family' }; if (`$f) { 'FONT-YES' } else { 'FONT-NO' }"
    $r = Invoke-PSCode -Code $code -NoProfile
    return ($r.Output -match 'FONT-YES')
}

function Get-PSModuleVersion {
    param([string]$Module, [string]$Exe)
    $code = "`$m = Get-Module -ListAvailable -Name '$Module' | Sort-Object Version -Descending | Select-Object -First 1; if (`$m) { 'MODVER=' + `$m.Version.ToString() } else { 'MODVER=' }"
    $r = Invoke-PSCode -Code $code -NoProfile -Exe $Exe
    $m = [regex]::Match($r.Output, 'MODVER=(\S*)')
    if ($m.Success -and $m.Groups[1].Value) { return $m.Groups[1].Value }
    return $null
}

function Get-ToolState {
    param($Tool)
    $s = [ordered]@{ installed = $false; version = $null; path = $null; via = $null; detail = '' }
    $detect = $Tool.detect
    if (-not $detect) {
        if ($Tool.exe) { $detect = 'exe' } elseif ($Tool.wingetId) { $detect = 'winget' } else { $detect = 'none' }
    }
    if ($detect -eq 'exe') {
        $path = $null
        if ($Tool.exePath) {
            $p = [Environment]::ExpandEnvironmentVariables($Tool.exePath)
            if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $p) { $path = $p }
        } else {
            $path = Resolve-Cmd $Tool.exe
        }
        if ($path) {
            $s.installed = $true; $s.path = $path; $s.via = 'exe'
            $s.version = Get-ToolVersionFromExe -Path $path -Tool $Tool
        }
        if ((-not $s.version) -and $Tool.wingetId) {
            $wv = Get-WingetInstalledVersion $Tool.wingetId
            if ($wv) { $s.installed = $true; $s.version = $wv; if (-not $s.via) { $s.via = 'winget' } }
        }
    } elseif ($detect -eq 'winget') {
        $wv = Get-WingetInstalledVersion $Tool.wingetId
        if ($wv) { $s.installed = $true; $s.version = $wv; $s.via = 'winget' }
        if ((-not $s.installed) -and $Tool.exe) {
            $path = Resolve-Cmd $Tool.exe
            if ($path) { $s.installed = $true; $s.path = $path; $s.via = 'exe'; $s.version = Get-ToolVersionFromExe -Path $path -Tool $Tool }
        }
    } elseif ($detect -eq 'appx') {
        try {
            $pk = @(Get-AppxPackage -Name $Tool.appxName -ErrorAction Stop) | Select-Object -First 1
            if ($pk) { $s.installed = $true; $s.version = [string]$pk.Version; $s.via = 'appx'; $s.path = $pk.InstallLocation }
        } catch { $s.detail = 'Get-AppxPackage failed: ' + $_.Exception.Message }
    } elseif ($detect -eq 'font') {
        $fam = $global:TK.Manifest.pins.font.family
        if (Test-FontFamily $fam) { $s.installed = $true; $s.version = $global:TK.Manifest.pins.font.release; $s.via = 'font'; $s.detail = $fam }
    } elseif ($detect -eq 'psmodule') {
        $v5 = Get-PSModuleVersion -Module $Tool.moduleName
        $pw = Resolve-Cmd 'pwsh'
        $v7 = $null
        if ($pw) { $v7 = Get-PSModuleVersion -Module $Tool.moduleName -Exe $pw }
        $s.detail = ('5.1={0} 7={1}' -f $v5, $v7)
        if ($v5) { $s.installed = $true; $s.version = $v5; $s.via = 'psmodule' }
        $s['version51'] = $v5; $s['version7'] = $v7; $s['pwshPresent'] = [bool]$pw
    } elseif ($detect -eq 'uv-python') {
        $uvp = [Environment]::ExpandEnvironmentVariables('%USERPROFILE%\.local\bin\uv.exe')
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $uvp) {
            $r = Invoke-Native -File $uvp -Arguments @('python', 'list', '--only-installed') -TimeoutSec 60
            $spec = $global:TK.Manifest.pins.python.uvSpec
            $m = [regex]::Match($r.Output, ('cpython-(' + [regex]::Escape($spec) + '\.\d+)'))
            if ($m.Success) { $s.installed = $true; $s.version = $m.Groups[1].Value; $s.via = 'uv-python' }
        }
    }
    if ($Tool.policy -eq 'absent' -and (-not $s.installed) -and $Tool.exe) {
        $path = Resolve-Cmd $Tool.exe
        if ($path) { $s.installed = $true; $s.path = $path; $s.via = 'exe' }
    }
    return [pscustomobject]$s
}

function Get-InstallInfo {
    param([string]$Path)
    $info = [ordered]@{ drive = $null; sizeMB = $null; root = $null }
    if (-not $Path) { return [pscustomobject]$info }
    try {
        $info.drive = ([IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($Path)).Substring(0, 2)
        $root = $null
        if ($Path -match '^(.*\\WinGet\\Packages\\[^\\]+)\\') { $root = $Matches[1] }
        elseif ($Path -match '^([A-Za-z]:\\Program Files(?: \(x86\))?\\[^\\]+)\\') { $root = $Matches[1] }
        if ($Path -match '\\WindowsApps\\') { $root = $null; $info.root = 'WindowsApps (not readable)'; return [pscustomobject]$info }
        if ($root -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $root)) {
            $sum = (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $root -Recurse -File -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Measure-Object Length -Sum).Sum
            $info.root = $root; $info.sizeMB = [math]::Round(([double]$sum) / 1MB, 1)
        } elseif (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Path -PathType Leaf) {
            $info.root = $Path; $info.sizeMB = [math]::Round((Get-Item -LiteralPath $Path).Length / 1MB, 1)
        }
    } catch { }
    return [pscustomobject]$info
}

function Read-ToolkitLock {
    $p = Join-Path $global:TK.Root 'toolkit.lock.json'
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $p) { return (Get-Content -LiteralPath $p -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json) }
    return $null
}

function Get-LockedVersion {
    param($Lock, [string]$Id)
    if (-not $Lock) { return $null }
    $t = $Lock.tools.$Id
    if ($t) { return $t.version }
    return $null
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- backups
function Backup-Target {
    param([string]$Path)
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Path -PathType Leaf)) { return $null }
    $base = 'D:\Backup\Toolkit'
    $dir = Join-Path $base $global:TK.Ts
    if ($global:TK.DryRun) { Write-Log ("[DRYRUN] would back up {0} to {1}" -f $Path, $dir); return '(dry run)' }
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null
    $flat = ($Path -replace '[:\\/ ]', '_')
    $dest = Join-Path $dir $flat
    Copy-Item -LiteralPath $Path -Destination $dest -Force
    $global:TK.Backups.Add([pscustomobject]@{ original = $Path; backup = $dest })
    $idx = Join-Path $dir 'backup-index.json'
    Write-Utf8NoBom -Path $idx -Text (ConvertTo-Json -InputObject @($global:TK.Backups) -Depth 4)
    Write-Log ("backup: {0} -> {1}" -f $Path, $dest)
    return $dest
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- planning
function Get-ToolPlan {
    # Decides what 10-install-toolkit would do for one tool. Returns action, version (pin) and reason.
    param($Tool, $State, $Lock)
    $p = [ordered]@{ action = 'none'; version = $null; reason = '' }
    $policy = $Tool.policy
    $lockVer = $null
    if ($Lock -and $Lock.host -ne $global:TK.Host) { $lockVer = Get-LockedVersion -Lock $Lock -Id $Tool.id }
    if ($policy -eq 'catalog-only') { $p.action = 'skip'; $p.reason = 'catalog only'; return [pscustomobject]$p }
    if ($policy -eq 'absent') {
        if ($State.installed) { $p.action = 'warn-present'; $p.reason = 'must not exist on this machine' } else { $p.reason = 'absent as required' }
        return [pscustomobject]$p
    }
    if ($policy -eq 'retire') {
        if ($State.installed) { $p.action = 'pending-retire'; $p.reason = $Tool.retireCommand } else { $p.reason = 'already retired' }
        return [pscustomobject]$p
    }
    if ($Tool.method -eq 'verify') {
        if ($State.installed) { $p.reason = 'present' } else { $p.action = 'missing'; $p.reason = 'verify-only tool is missing' }
        return [pscustomobject]$p
    }
    if ($Tool.method -eq 'winget') {
        foreach ($c in @($Tool.conflictsWith)) {
            if ($c -and (Get-WingetInstalledVersion $c)) {
                $p.action = 'manual'; $p.reason = ("{0} is installed; run {1} by hand" -f $c, $Tool.manualScript)
                return [pscustomobject]$p
            }
        }
        if (-not $State.installed) { $p.action = 'install'; $p.version = $lockVer; $p.reason = 'not installed'; return [pscustomobject]$p }
        if ($Tool.expectMajor -and $State.version) {
            $maj = [int](($State.version -split '\.')[0])
            if ($maj -ne [int]$Tool.expectMajor) { $p.action = 'manual'; $p.reason = ("major {0} installed, {1} wanted; run {2} by hand" -f $maj, $Tool.expectMajor, $Tool.manualScript); return [pscustomobject]$p }
        }
        if ($policy -eq 'latest') { $p.action = 'upgrade'; $p.reason = 'policy latest' } else { $p.reason = 'present, adopted' }
        return [pscustomobject]$p
    }
    if ($Tool.method -eq 'npm') {
        if (-not $State.installed) { $p.action = 'install'; $p.version = $lockVer; $p.reason = 'not installed' } else { $p.reason = 'present, adopted' }
        return [pscustomobject]$p
    }
    if ($Tool.method -eq 'uv-standalone') {
        $pin = $global:TK.Manifest.pins.uv.version
        if ($State.installed -and $State.version -eq $pin) { $p.reason = 'standalone uv at pin' }
        else { $p.action = 'install'; $p.version = $pin; $p.reason = 'standalone uv missing or not at pin' }
        return [pscustomobject]$p
    }
    if ($Tool.method -eq 'uv-python') {
        if (-not $State.installed) { $p.action = 'install'; $p.reason = 'no uv-managed python' } else { $p.reason = 'present' }
        return [pscustomobject]$p
    }
    if ($Tool.method -eq 'font') {
        if (-not $State.installed) { $p.action = 'install'; $p.reason = 'family not registered' } else { $p.reason = 'family registered' }
        return [pscustomobject]$p
    }
    if ($Tool.method -eq 'psmodule') {
        $need5 = (-not $State.installed)
        $need7 = ($State.pwshPresent -and (-not $State.version7))
        if ($need5 -or $need7) { $p.action = 'install'; $p.reason = ("5.1 missing={0} 7 missing={1}" -f $need5, $need7) } else { $p.reason = 'present in both editions' }
        return [pscustomobject]$p
    }
    return [pscustomobject]$p
}

```
- `Write` -- `D:\Users\_Anant\10_Areas\Documents\Jarvis\40_Resources\CS\Concepts\New Laptop\Old Laptop Rebuild\Windows Toolkit\_scripts\00-preflight.ps1`
```
<#
00-preflight.ps1 - READ-ONLY. Run first, on either laptop, in a normal (not elevated) shell.
Detects the machine and the vault, checks the manifest and every script, compares the manifest with reality,
prints the plan that 10-install-toolkit.ps1 would carry out, and scans the config sources for secrets.
Nothing is installed or changed. Writes only a log and a result JSON.
#>
param([ValidateSet('dell', 'acer')][string]$Machine)

. "$PSScriptRoot\_lib.ps1"
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Initialize-Toolkit -Name '00-preflight' -DryRun $false -Machine $Machine | Out-Null
$m = $global:TK.Manifest
$role = $global:TK.Role

try {
    # ---- 1 environment facts
    Write-Log '--- 1 Environment' 'STEP'
    $c = Get-PSDrive C; $d = Get-PSDrive D -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    $freeC = [math]::Round($c.Free / 1GB, 1)
    $freeD = 0
    if ($d) { $freeD = [math]::Round($d.Free / 1GB, 1) }
    Add-Result 'env-drives' 'INFO' ("C: free {0} GiB, D: free {1} GiB" -f $freeC, $freeD) @{ freeC = $freeC; freeD = $freeD }
    Add-Result 'env-powershell' 'INFO' ("PowerShell {0}, admin={1}, execution policy (effective) {2}" -f $PSVersionTable.PSVersion, (Test-IsAdmin), (Get-ExecutionPolicy))
    if (Test-IsAdmin) { Add-Result 'env-elevation' 'WARN' 'Running elevated. Installers and config writes are meant to run in a normal shell.' }
    $wg = Resolve-Cmd 'winget'
    if ($wg) { Add-Result 'env-winget' 'PASS' ("winget at {0}" -f $wg) } else { Add-Result 'env-winget' 'FAIL' 'winget not found' }
    Add-Result 'env-vault' 'PASS' ("vault root {0}" -f $global:TK.Vault) @{ vaultRoot = $global:TK.Vault }
    Add-Result 'env-machine' 'PASS' ("{0} ({1}) detected" -f $role, $m.machines.$role.label)
    try {
        $r = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri 'https://github.com' -Method Head -UseBasicParsing -TimeoutSec 20
        Add-Result 'env-network' 'PASS' ("github.com reachable (HTTP {0})" -f $r.StatusCode)
    } catch { Add-Result 'env-network' 'WARN' 'github.com not reachable; font and uv downloads would fail' }

    # ---- 2 scripts: ASCII and parse
    Write-Log '--- 2 Scripts parse and ASCII check' 'STEP'
    foreach ($f in Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $global:TKLibDir -Filter '*.ps1' | Sort-Object Name) {
        $bytes = [IO.File]::ReadAllBytes($f.FullName)
        $bad = 0; foreach ($b in $bytes) { if ($b -gt 127) { $bad++ } }
        $errs = $null; $tokens = $null
        [System.Management.Automation.Language.Parser]::ParseFile($f.FullName, [ref]$tokens, [ref]$errs) | Out-Null
        $n = 0; if ($errs) { $n = $errs.Count }
        if ($bad -eq 0 -and $n -eq 0) { Add-Result ('script-' + $f.Name) 'PASS' 'ASCII only, parses clean' }
        else { Add-Result ('script-' + $f.Name) 'FAIL' ("non-ASCII bytes={0}, parse errors={1}" -f $bad, $n) }
    }

    # ---- 3 manifest sanity and config sources
    Write-Log '--- 3 Manifest and config sources' 'STEP'
    $ids = @($m.tools | ForEach-Object { $_.id })
    $dups = @($ids | Group-Object | Where-Object { $_.Count -gt 1 })
    if ($dups.Count -eq 0) { Add-Result 'manifest-ids' 'PASS' ("{0} tools, ids unique" -f $ids.Count) } else { Add-Result 'manifest-ids' 'FAIL' 'duplicate ids' }
    foreach ($cfg in $m.configs) {
        if (-not $cfg.source) { continue }
        $sp = Join-Path $global:TK.Root $cfg.source
        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $sp)) { Add-Result ('config-src-' + $cfg.id) 'FAIL' ("missing source {0}" -f $cfg.source); continue }
        $text = [IO.File]::ReadAllText($sp)
        $hits = Get-SecretHitCount $text
        $hash = Get-FileSha256 $sp
        $st = 'PASS'; $dt = ("sha256 {0}..." -f $hash.Substring(0, 12))
        if ($hits -gt 0) { $st = 'FAIL'; $dt = ("secret-like patterns matched: {0} (text not shown)" -f $hits) }
        elseif ($cfg.sha256 -and ($cfg.sha256 -ne $hash)) { $st = 'FAIL'; $dt = ("hash differs from manifest ({0})" -f $hash) }
        elseif ($cfg.sha256) { $dt += ' matches the Acer snapshot hash' }
        Add-Result ('config-src-' + $cfg.id) $st $dt @{ sha256 = $hash }
    }

    # ---- 4 existing profiles, terminal, git on THIS machine
    Write-Log '--- 4 Existing files that will be touched' 'STEP'
    foreach ($cfg in $m.configs | Where-Object { $_.kind -eq 'profile-block' }) {
        $tp = Expand-ToolkitPath $cfg.target $global:TK.Vault
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $tp) {
            $txt = [IO.File]::ReadAllText($tp)
            $hits = Get-SecretHitCount $txt
            $has = $txt.Contains($global:TKBlockBegin)
            $errs = $null; $tokens = $null
            [System.Management.Automation.Language.Parser]::ParseInput($txt, [ref]$tokens, [ref]$errs) | Out-Null
            $n = 0; if ($errs) { $n = $errs.Count }
            $detail = ("{0} exists, {1} bytes, sha256 {2}..., managed block present={3}, parse errors={4}, secret-like hits={5}" -f $tp, $txt.Length, (Get-FileSha256 $tp).Substring(0, 12), $has, $n, $hits)
            $st = 'INFO'; if ($n -gt 0 -or $hits -gt 0) { $st = 'WARN' }
            Add-Result ('profile-' + $cfg.id) $st $detail @{ path = $tp; sha256 = (Get-FileSha256 $tp) }
        } else {
            Add-Result ('profile-' + $cfg.id) 'INFO' ("{0} does not exist yet; it will be created" -f $tp) @{ path = $tp }
        }
    }
    $wtDir = Get-ChildItem (Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'Packages') -Filter 'Microsoft.WindowsTerminal_*' -Directory -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -First 1
    if ($wtDir) {
        $wtp = Join-Path $wtDir.FullName 'LocalState\settings.json'
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $wtp) {
            $ok = $true; try { $null = Get-Content -LiteralPath $wtp -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json } catch { $ok = $false }
            if ($ok) { Add-Result 'terminal-settings' 'INFO' ("{0} parses as JSON, sha256 {1}..." -f $wtp, (Get-FileSha256 $wtp).Substring(0, 12)) @{ path = $wtp } }
            else { Add-Result 'terminal-settings' 'WARN' 'settings.json has comments or is not strict JSON; 20-apply-configs will report MANUAL and not touch it' @{ path = $wtp } }
        } else { Add-Result 'terminal-settings' 'INFO' 'no settings.json yet (open Windows Terminal once)' }
    } else { Add-Result 'terminal-settings' 'WARN' 'Windows Terminal package folder not found' }
    $gp = Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE '.gitconfig'
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $gp) {
        $git = Resolve-Cmd 'git'
        if ($git) {
            $r = Invoke-Native -File $git -Arguments @('config', '--global', '--get', 'core.autocrlf') -TimeoutSec 20
            Add-Result 'git-global' 'INFO' ("global .gitconfig present; core.autocrlf={0} (will become input)" -f $r.Output.Trim())
        }
    } else { Add-Result 'git-global' 'INFO' 'no global .gitconfig yet' }

    # ---- 5 manifest vs reality (the plan)
    Write-Log '--- 5 Manifest versus reality (the plan for 10-install-toolkit)' 'STEP'
    $lock = Read-ToolkitLock
    if ($lock) { Add-Result 'lock' 'INFO' ("toolkit.lock.json present (host {0}, {1})" -f $lock.host, $lock.created) } else { Add-Result 'lock' 'INFO' 'no toolkit.lock.json yet (this host will create it after 99-verify -WriteLock)' }
    $planned = 0
    foreach ($t in $m.tools) {
        if (-not (Test-ToolOnMachine $t $role) -and $t.policy -ne 'catalog-only') { continue }
        if ($t.policy -eq 'catalog-only') { continue }
        $st = Get-ToolState $t
        $pl = Get-ToolPlan -Tool $t -State $st -Lock $lock
        $status = 'OK'
        if ($pl.action -in 'install', 'upgrade') { $status = 'PLAN'; $planned++ }
        elseif ($pl.action -eq 'manual') { $status = 'MANUAL' }
        elseif ($pl.action -in 'pending-retire') { $status = 'PENDING' }
        elseif ($pl.action -in 'missing', 'warn-present') { $status = 'WARN' }
        $ver = $st.version; if (-not $ver) { $ver = '-' }
        $act = $pl.action; if ($pl.version) { $act = $act + ' ' + $pl.version }
        Add-Result $t.id $status ("installed={0} version={1} -> {2} ({3})" -f $st.installed, $ver, $act, $pl.reason) @{ installed = $st.installed; version = $st.version; path = $st.path; action = $pl.action }
    }
    Add-Result 'plan-total' 'INFO' ("{0} install/upgrade action(s) planned for 10-install-toolkit.ps1" -f $planned)

    # ---- 6 environment variables (known, non-secret names only)
    Write-Log '--- 6 Environment variables' 'STEP'
    foreach ($e in $m.env.verify) {
        $v = [Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable($e.name, 'User')
        $want = $e.dell
        if ($role -eq 'dell' -and $want) {
            if ($v -eq $want) { Add-Result ('env-' + $e.name) 'PASS' ("{0}={1}" -f $e.name, $v) } else { Add-Result ('env-' + $e.name) 'WARN' ("{0} is '{1}', expected '{2}' (Build 3 variable, not changed here)" -f $e.name, $v, $want) }
        } else {
            $shown = $v; if (-not $shown) { $shown = '(unset)' }
            Add-Result ('env-' + $e.name) 'INFO' ("{0}={1}" -f $e.name, $shown)
        }
    }
    foreach ($e in $m.env.set) {
        $v = [Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable($e.name, 'User')
        $shown = $v; if (-not $shown) { $shown = '(unset, will be set by 20-apply-configs)' }
        Add-Result ('env-' + $e.name) 'INFO' ("{0}={1}" -f $e.name, $shown)
    }

    # ---- 7 junctions
    foreach ($t in $m.tools | Where-Object { $_.junction -and (Test-ToolOnMachine $_ $role) }) {
        $jp = Expand-ToolkitPath $t.junction.path $global:TK.Vault
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $jp) {
            $it = Get-Item -LiteralPath $jp -Force
            $isLink = [bool]($it.Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint)
            $tgt = ''; try { $tgt = ($it.Target -join ';') } catch { }
            if ($isLink) { Add-Result ('junction-' + $t.id) 'PASS' ("{0} -> {1}" -f $jp, $tgt) } else { Add-Result ('junction-' + $t.id) 'WARN' ("{0} exists but is not a junction" -f $jp) }
        } else { Add-Result ('junction-' + $t.id) 'WARN' ("{0} missing" -f $jp) }
    }
    $ctx = @{ plannedActions = $planned; freeC = $freeC; freeD = $freeD }
} catch {
    Add-Result 'preflight-error' 'FAIL' $_.Exception.Message
    $ctx = @{}
}
Complete-Toolkit -Summary $ctx

```

- `Write` -- `D:\Users\_Anant\10_Areas\Documents\Jarvis\40_Resources\CS\Concepts\New Laptop\Old Laptop Rebuild\Windows Toolkit\_scripts\10-install-toolkit.ps1`
```
<#
10-install-toolkit.ps1 - installs and adopts the toolkit from toolkit.json. Run in a NORMAL (not elevated) shell.
Windows will show UAC prompts for the machine-wide installers (Git, GitHub CLI, Starship, Node); click Yes.
  -DryRun   print the plan, change nothing
  -Only     limit to some tool ids, for example -Only starship,fzf
  -Machine  force dell or acer if detection fails
Safe to run twice. Continues past a failed package and prints a summary table. Exit code 1 only on a required failure.
Never installs Tailscale, never touches Node 22 (use 11-node-lts-swap.ps1), never removes anything.
#>
param([switch]$DryRun, [ValidateSet('dell', 'acer')][string]$Machine, [string[]]$Only)

. "$PSScriptRoot\_lib.ps1"
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Initialize-Toolkit -Name '10-install-toolkit' -DryRun $DryRun.IsPresent -Machine $Machine | Out-Null
$m = $global:TK.Manifest
$role = $global:TK.Role
$dry = $global:TK.DryRun
$lock = Read-ToolkitLock

function Add-ToolInfo {
    param($Tool, $State)
    $info = Get-InstallInfo $State.path
    return @{ version = $State.version; path = $State.path; drive = $info.drive; sizeMB = $info.sizeMB; policy = $Tool.policy }
}

function Repair-PortablePath {
    param($Tool)
    $pkgRoot = Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'Microsoft\WinGet\Packages'
    $pkg = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $pkgRoot -Directory -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Where-Object { $_.Name -like ($Tool.wingetId + '_*') } | Select-Object -First 1
    if (-not $pkg) { Write-Log ("no package folder found for {0}" -f $Tool.wingetId) 'WARN'; return $false }
    $exe = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $pkg.FullName -Recurse -Filter ($Tool.exe + '.exe') -File -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -First 1
    if (-not $exe) { Write-Log ("{0}.exe not found under {1}" -f $Tool.exe, $pkg.FullName) 'WARN'; return $false }
    $links = Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'Microsoft\WinGet\Links'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $links | Out-Null
    $link = Join-Path $links ($Tool.exe + '.exe')
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $link)) {
        New-Item -ItemType HardLink -Path $link -Value $exe.FullName | Out-Null
        Write-Log ("hard link created: {0} -> {1}" -f $link, $exe.FullName)
    }
    $up = [Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable('Path', 'User')
    $has = $false
    foreach ($e in ($up -split ';')) { if ($e.TrimEnd('\') -ieq $links.TrimEnd('\')) { $has = $true } }
    if (-not $has) {
        [Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable('Path', ($links + ';' + $up), 'User')
        Write-Log ("added {0} to the user PATH" -f $links)
    }
    Update-SessionPath
    return $true
}

function Install-WingetTool {
    param($Tool, $Plan)
    $r = Invoke-WingetAction -Action 'install' -Id $Tool.wingetId -Version $Plan.version
    Update-SessionPath
    $st = Get-ToolState $Tool
    if ((-not $st.installed) -and $Plan.version) {
        Write-Log ("pinned version {0} failed; retrying with the current version" -f $Plan.version) 'WARN'
        $r = Invoke-WingetAction -Action 'install' -Id $Tool.wingetId -Version $null
        Update-SessionPath
        $st = Get-ToolState $Tool
    }
    if ((-not $st.installed) -and $Tool.portable) {
        if (Repair-PortablePath $Tool) { $st = Get-ToolState $Tool }
    }
    if ($st.installed) { Add-Result $Tool.id 'INSTALLED' ("version {0} (winget exit {1})" -f $st.version, $r.ExitCode) (Add-ToolInfo $Tool $st) }
    else { Add-Result $Tool.id 'FAIL' ("not detected after install (winget exit {0}); open a new shell and re-run" -f $r.ExitCode) }
}

function Update-WingetTool {
    param($Tool, $State)
    $before = $State.version
    $r = Invoke-WingetAction -Action 'upgrade' -Id $Tool.wingetId -Version $null
    Update-SessionPath
    $st = Get-ToolState $Tool
    if ($st.installed -and $st.version -ne $before) { Add-Result $Tool.id 'UPGRADED' ("{0} -> {1}" -f $before, $st.version) (Add-ToolInfo $Tool $st) }
    elseif ($st.installed) { Add-Result $Tool.id 'OK' ("already current at {0} (winget exit {1})" -f $st.version, $r.ExitCode) (Add-ToolInfo $Tool $st) }
    else { Add-Result $Tool.id 'FAIL' ("tool not detected after upgrade (winget exit {0})" -f $r.ExitCode) }
}

function Install-NpmTool {
    param($Tool, $Plan)
    $npm = Resolve-Cmd 'npm'
    if (-not $npm) { Add-Result $Tool.id 'FAIL' 'npm not found (Node missing)'; return }
    $spec = $Tool.npmPackage
    if ($Plan.version) { $spec = $spec + '@' + $Plan.version }
    Write-Log ("npm install -g {0}" -f $spec)
    $r = Invoke-Native -File $npm -Arguments @('install', '-g', $spec) -TimeoutSec 900
    Write-NativeTail $r
    Update-SessionPath
    $st = Get-ToolState $Tool
    if (-not $st.installed) {
        $gp = Join-Path $env:APPDATA 'npm'
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $gp) { $env:Path = $env:Path + ';' + $gp; $st = Get-ToolState $Tool }
    }
    if ($st.installed) { Add-Result $Tool.id 'INSTALLED' ("npm exit {0}, version {1}" -f $r.ExitCode, $st.version) (Add-ToolInfo $Tool $st) }
    else { Add-Result $Tool.id 'FAIL' ("not detected after npm install (exit {0})" -f $r.ExitCode) }
}

function Install-UvStandalone {
    param($Tool)
    $pin = $m.pins.uv
    $dir = Join-Path $env:TEMP ("toolkit-uv-" + $global:TK.Ts)
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null
    $f = Join-Path $dir 'uv-installer.ps1'
    try {
        Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $pin.installerUrl -OutFile $f -UseBasicParsing
    } catch { Add-Result $Tool.id 'FAIL' ("download failed: " + $_.Exception.Message); return }
    $h = Get-FileSha256 $f
    if ($h -ne $pin.installerSha256.ToUpper()) { Add-Result $Tool.id 'FAIL' ("installer SHA-256 mismatch: got {0}" -f $h); return }
    Write-Log ("uv installer verified, sha256 {0}" -f $h)
    $r = Invoke-Native -File $f -Arguments @() -TimeoutSec 600 -Env @{ UV_NO_MODIFY_PATH = '1' }
    Write-NativeTail $r
    $bin = Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE '.local\bin'
    $up = [Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable('Path', 'User')
    $has = $false
    foreach ($e in ($up -split ';')) { if ([Environment]::ExpandEnvironmentVariables($e).TrimEnd('\') -ieq $bin.TrimEnd('\')) { $has = $true } }
    if (-not $has) {
        [Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable('Path', ($bin + ';' + $up), 'User')
        Write-Log ("added {0} to the user PATH" -f $bin)
    }
    Update-SessionPath
    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $dir -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    $st = Get-ToolState $Tool
    if ($st.installed -and $st.version -eq $pin.version) { Add-Result $Tool.id 'INSTALLED' ("standalone uv {0} at {1}" -f $st.version, $st.path) (Add-ToolInfo $Tool $st) }
    else { Add-Result $Tool.id 'FAIL' ("uv not at pin after install (found {0}, installer exit {1})" -f $st.version, $r.ExitCode) }
}

function Install-UvPython {
    param($Tool)
    $uv = [Environment]::ExpandEnvironmentVariables('%USERPROFILE%\.local\bin\uv.exe')
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $uv)) { Add-Result $Tool.id 'FAIL' 'standalone uv missing, cannot install Python'; return }
    $spec = $m.pins.python.uvSpec
    $r = Invoke-Native -File $uv -Arguments @('python', 'install', $spec) -TimeoutSec 900
    Write-NativeTail $r
    $st = Get-ToolState $Tool
    if ($st.installed) { Add-Result $Tool.id 'INSTALLED' ("cpython {0} (uv exit {1})" -f $st.version, $r.ExitCode) @{ version = $st.version } }
    else { Add-Result $Tool.id 'FAIL' ("no uv-managed python found after install (exit {0})" -f $r.ExitCode) }
}

function Install-Font {
    param($Tool)
    $pin = $m.pins.font
    $work = Join-Path $env:TEMP ("toolkit-font-" + $global:TK.Ts)
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $work | Out-Null
    $arc = Join-Path $work 'IosevkaTerm.tar.xz'
    $out = Join-Path $work 'ttf'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $out | Out-Null
    Write-Log ("downloading {0} (about 87.5 MB)" -f $pin.url)
    try { Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $pin.url -OutFile $arc -UseBasicParsing } catch { Add-Result $Tool.id 'FAIL' ("download failed: " + $_.Exception.Message); return }
    $h = (Get-FileSha256 $arc)
    if ($h -ne $pin.sha256.ToUpper()) { Add-Result $Tool.id 'FAIL' ("SHA-256 mismatch: got {0}" -f $h); return }
    Write-Log 'archive SHA-256 verified'
    $tar = (Get-Command tar.exe).Source
    $r = Invoke-Native -File $tar -Arguments @('-xf', $arc, '-C', $out, $pin.filePattern) -TimeoutSec 300
    $ttf = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $out -Filter '*.ttf' -File)
    if ($ttf.Count -lt 20) { Add-Result $Tool.id 'FAIL' ("extracted only {0} TTF files (tar exit {1}: {2})" -f $ttf.Count, $r.ExitCode, (ConvertTo-Ascii $r.Error)); return }
    $fontDir = Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'Microsoft\Windows\Fonts'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $fontDir | Out-Null
    $reg = 'HKCU:\Software\Microsoft\Windows NT\CurrentVersion\Fonts'
    if (-not (Test-Path $reg)) { New-Item -Path $reg -Force | Out-Null }
    $total = 0.0
    foreach ($f in $ttf) {
        $dest = Join-Path $fontDir $f.Name
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $dest -Force
        New-ItemProperty -Path $reg -Name ([IO.Path]::GetFileNameWithoutExtension($f.Name) + ' (TrueType)') -Value $dest -PropertyType String -Force | Out-Null
        $total += $f.Length
    }
    if (-not ('ToolkitNative.Win32' -as [type])) {
        Add-Type -Namespace ToolkitNative -Name Win32 -MemberDefinition '[System.Runtime.InteropServices.DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError=true, CharSet=System.Runtime.InteropServices.CharSet.Auto)] public static extern System.IntPtr SendMessageTimeout(System.IntPtr hWnd, uint Msg, System.UIntPtr wParam, System.IntPtr lParam, uint fuFlags, uint uTimeout, out System.UIntPtr lpdwResult);'
    }
    $res = [UIntPtr]::Zero
    [ToolkitNative.Win32]::SendMessageTimeout([IntPtr]0xffff, 0x1D, [UIntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero, 2, 2000, [ref]$res) | Out-Null
    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $work -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    $sizeMB = [math]::Round($total / 1MB, 1)
    if (Test-FontFamily $pin.family) { Add-Result $Tool.id 'INSTALLED' ("{0} TTF files, {1} MB, family '{2}' registered" -f $ttf.Count, $sizeMB, $pin.family) @{ drive = 'C:'; sizeMB = $sizeMB; version = $pin.release } }
    else { Add-Result $Tool.id 'WARN' ("{0} files copied ({1} MB) but family '{2}' is not visible yet; sign out and back in, then run 99-verify" -f $ttf.Count, $sizeMB, $pin.family) @{ drive = 'C:'; sizeMB = $sizeMB } }
}

function Install-PsModule {
    param($Tool, $State)
    $ver = $m.pins.psfzf.version
    $core = "Install-Module -Name '{0}' -RequiredVersion '{1}' -Scope CurrentUser -Repository PSGallery -Force -AllowClobber -Confirm:`$false -ErrorAction Stop; 'MOD-DONE'" -f $Tool.moduleName, $ver
    if (-not $State.installed) {
        $code = "[Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol = [Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol -bor [Net.SecurityProtocolType]::Tls12; if (-not (Get-PackageProvider -Name NuGet -ListAvailable -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) { Install-PackageProvider -Name NuGet -MinimumVersion 2.8.5.201 -Scope CurrentUser -Force | Out-Null }; " + $core
        $r = Invoke-PSCode -Code $code -NoProfile -TimeoutSec 600
        Write-NativeTail $r 6
    }
    $pw = Resolve-Cmd 'pwsh'
    if ($pw -and (-not $State.version7)) {
        $r2 = Invoke-PSCode -Code $core -NoProfile -TimeoutSec 600 -Exe $pw
        Write-NativeTail $r2 6
    }
    $st = Get-ToolState $Tool
    if ($st.installed -and ((-not $st.pwshPresent) -or $st.version7)) { Add-Result $Tool.id 'INSTALLED' ("PSFzf {0} ({1})" -f $st.version, $st.detail) @{ version = $st.version } }
    else { Add-Result $Tool.id 'FAIL' ("PSFzf not present in every edition: {0}" -f $st.detail) }
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ pass 1: plan
Write-Log '--- Plan' 'STEP'
$rows = @()
foreach ($t in $m.tools) {
    if ($t.policy -eq 'catalog-only') { continue }
    if (-not (Test-ToolOnMachine $t $role)) { continue }
    if ($Only -and ($Only -notcontains $t.id)) { continue }
    $st = Get-ToolState $t
    $pl = Get-ToolPlan -Tool $t -State $st -Lock $lock
    $rows += [pscustomobject]@{ Tool = $t; State = $st; Plan = $pl }
    $v = $st.version; if (-not $v) { $v = '-' }
    Write-Log ("{0,-18} installed={1,-5} version={2,-14} action={3} ({4})" -f $t.id, $st.installed, $v, $pl.action, $pl.reason)
}
$todo = @($rows | Where-Object { $_.Plan.action -in 'install', 'upgrade' })
Write-Log ("Planned total: {0} install/upgrade action(s)" -f $todo.Count) 'STEP'
$uac = @($todo | Where-Object { $_.Tool.uac } | ForEach-Object { $_.Tool.id })
if ($uac.Count -gt 0) { Write-Log ("UAC prompts expected for: {0}. Click Yes on each." -f ($uac -join ', ')) 'WARN' }

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ pass 2: execute (state re-read per tool)
Write-Log '--- Execute' 'STEP'
foreach ($row in $rows) {
    $t = $row.Tool
    try {
        $st = Get-ToolState $t
        $pl = Get-ToolPlan -Tool $t -State $st -Lock $lock
        $action = $pl.action
        if ($dry) {
            if ($action -in 'install', 'upgrade') {
                $ver = ''; if ($pl.version) { $ver = ' ' + $pl.version }
                Add-Result $t.id 'PLAN' ("[DRYRUN] would {0}{1} ({2})" -f $action, $ver, $pl.reason)
            } elseif ($action -eq 'missing' -and $t.dependsOn) {
                Add-Result $t.id 'PLAN' ("[DRYRUN] available after {0} is installed" -f $t.dependsOn)
            } elseif ($action -eq 'missing') { Add-Result $t.id 'MANUAL' ("missing; install by hand ({0})" -f $t.name) }
            elseif ($action -eq 'manual') { Add-Result $t.id 'MANUAL' $pl.reason }
            elseif ($action -eq 'pending-retire') { Add-Result $t.id 'PENDING' $pl.reason }
            elseif ($action -eq 'warn-present') { Add-Result $t.id 'WARN' $pl.reason }
            else { Add-Result $t.id 'OK' ("{0} (version {1})" -f $pl.reason, $st.version) (Add-ToolInfo $t $st) }
            continue
        }
        switch ($action) {
            'install' {
                if ($t.method -eq 'winget') { Install-WingetTool $t $pl }
                elseif ($t.method -eq 'npm') { Install-NpmTool $t $pl }
                elseif ($t.method -eq 'uv-standalone') { Install-UvStandalone $t }
                elseif ($t.method -eq 'uv-python') { Install-UvPython $t }
                elseif ($t.method -eq 'font') { Install-Font $t }
                elseif ($t.method -eq 'psmodule') { Install-PsModule $t $st }
                else { Add-Result $t.id 'FAIL' ("no installer for method {0}" -f $t.method) }
            }
            'upgrade' { Update-WingetTool $t $st }
            'manual' { Add-Result $t.id 'MANUAL' $pl.reason }
            'missing' { Add-Result $t.id 'MANUAL' ("missing; install by hand ({0})" -f $t.name) }
            'pending-retire' { Add-Result $t.id 'PENDING' $pl.reason }
            'warn-present' { Add-Result $t.id 'WARN' $pl.reason }
            default { Add-Result $t.id 'OK' ("{0} (version {1})" -f $pl.reason, $st.version) (Add-ToolInfo $t $st) }
        }
    } catch {
        Add-Result $t.id 'FAIL' ("exception: " + $_.Exception.Message)
    }
}
Complete-Toolkit -Summary @{ plannedActions = $todo.Count }

```
- `Write` -- `D:\Users\_Anant\10_Areas\Documents\Jarvis\40_Resources\CS\Concepts\New Laptop\Old Laptop Rebuild\Windows Toolkit\_scripts\11-node-lts-swap.ps1`
```
<#
11-node-lts-swap.ps1 - Dell only, by hand. Replaces Node.js 22 (winget OpenJS.NodeJS.22) with Node.js LTS (OpenJS.NodeJS.LTS, 24.x).
Default is a DRY RUN. Add -Apply to change anything. Run in a NORMAL shell; Windows asks for UAC twice (uninstall, install).
Before -Apply: close VS Code, Cursor, Kiro, Antigravity, Claude Desktop and every terminal that runs node, npm or a dev server.
npm global packages live in %APPDATA%\npm and survive; the script lists them before and after to prove it.
Exit codes are printed (1605 = already gone, 3010 = reboot needed).
#>
param([switch]$Apply, [switch]$IgnoreRunning, [ValidateSet('dell', 'acer')][string]$Machine)

. "$PSScriptRoot\_lib.ps1"
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Initialize-Toolkit -Name '11-node-lts-swap' -DryRun (-not $Apply.IsPresent) -Machine $Machine | Out-Null
$dry = $global:TK.DryRun
$tool = $global:TK.Manifest.tools | Where-Object { $_.id -eq 'node' }

function Get-NpmGlobals {
    $npm = Resolve-Cmd 'npm'
    if (-not $npm) { return @() }
    $r = Invoke-Native -File $npm -Arguments @('ls', '-g', '--depth=0', '--json') -TimeoutSec 120
    try {
        $j = $r.Output | ConvertFrom-Json
        if ($j.dependencies) { return @($j.dependencies.PSObject.Properties | ForEach-Object { '{0}@{1}' -f $_.Name, $_.Value.version }) }
    } catch { }
    return @()
}

try {
    $v22 = Get-WingetInstalledVersion 'OpenJS.NodeJS.22'
    $vLts = Get-WingetInstalledVersion 'OpenJS.NodeJS.LTS'
    Add-Result 'node-state' 'INFO' ("OpenJS.NodeJS.22 = {0}; OpenJS.NodeJS.LTS = {1}" -f $v22, $vLts)
    $before = Get-NpmGlobals
    Add-Result 'npm-globals-before' 'INFO' ($before -join ', ') @{ globals = $before }

    if (-not $v22 -and $vLts) { Add-Result 'node-swap' 'OK' ("Node LTS {0} already installed and Node 22 is gone" -f $vLts); Complete-Toolkit }
    if (-not $v22 -and -not $vLts) { Add-Result 'node-swap' 'MANUAL' 'Neither Node 22 nor LTS was installed through winget. Run 10-install-toolkit.ps1 to install LTS.'; Complete-Toolkit }

    $running = @(Get-Process -Name node -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
    if ($running.Count -gt 0) {
        $names = ($running | ForEach-Object { 'node.exe pid ' + $_.Id }) -join ', '
        if ($IgnoreRunning) { Add-Result 'node-running' 'WARN' ("{0} (continuing because -IgnoreRunning)" -f $names) }
        else { Add-Result 'node-running' 'FAIL' ("{0}. Close editors, Claude Desktop and terminals running node, then re-run." -f $names); Complete-Toolkit }
    } else { Add-Result 'node-running' 'PASS' 'no node.exe running' }

    if ($dry) {
        Add-Result 'node-swap' 'PLAN' '[DRYRUN] would: winget uninstall OpenJS.NodeJS.22, then winget install OpenJS.NodeJS.LTS, then verify major 24 and the npm globals. Re-run with -Apply.'
        Complete-Toolkit
    }

    Write-Log 'Uninstalling Node 22 (a UAC prompt appears).' 'STEP'
    $r1 = Invoke-WingetAction -Action 'uninstall' -Id 'OpenJS.NodeJS.22' -Version $null
    Update-SessionPath
    $still = Get-WingetInstalledVersion 'OpenJS.NodeJS.22'
    if ($still) { Add-Result 'node22-uninstall' 'FAIL' ("Node 22 is still installed ({0}); winget exit {1}. Nothing else was changed." -f $still, $r1.ExitCode); Complete-Toolkit }
    Add-Result 'node22-uninstall' 'PASS' ("Node 22 removed (winget exit {0})" -f $r1.ExitCode)

    Write-Log 'Installing Node LTS (a UAC prompt appears).' 'STEP'
    $r2 = Invoke-WingetAction -Action 'install' -Id 'OpenJS.NodeJS.LTS' -Version $null
    Update-SessionPath
    $st = Get-ToolState $tool
    if ($st.installed -and $st.version -and ([int](($st.version -split '\.')[0]) -eq 24)) { Add-Result 'node-lts-install' 'PASS' ("node {0} at {1} (winget exit {2})" -f $st.version, $st.path, $r2.ExitCode) }
    else { Add-Result 'node-lts-install' 'FAIL' ("expected Node 24, found '{0}' (winget exit {1}). Open a new shell and run 10-install-toolkit.ps1 -Only node." -f $st.version, $r2.ExitCode) }

    $after = Get-NpmGlobals
    $lost = @($before | Where-Object { $after -notcontains $_ })
    if ($lost.Count -eq 0) { Add-Result 'npm-globals-after' 'PASS' ("all {0} global packages survived: {1}" -f $after.Count, ($after -join ', ')) @{ globals = $after } }
    else { Add-Result 'npm-globals-after' 'FAIL' ("missing after swap: {0}" -f ($lost -join ', ')) @{ globals = $after } }
} catch {
    Add-Result 'node-swap-error' 'FAIL' $_.Exception.Message
}
Complete-Toolkit

```
